# Tile Treemap

> Divide a single box into proportionally sized, color-coded tiles.

- Kind: component
- Docs: https://www.huanngdev.site/components/tile-treemap
- Registry: https://www.huanngdev.site/r/tile-treemap.json
- Apply: copy each file to the same path from the repository root. Leave the bytes unchanged.

## Read order

1. packages/core/src/components/tile-treemap.utils.ts
2. packages/core/src/components/tile-treemap.tsx

## packages/core/src/components/tile-treemap.utils.ts
```ts
export type TreemapRect = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
};

type WeightedItem = {
  index: number;
  area: number;
};

const EMPTY_RECT: TreemapRect = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };

export function toPercentages(values: number[]): number[] {
  const total = values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + Math.max(value, 0), 0);
  if (total <= 0) return values.map(() => 0);
  return values.map((value) => (Math.max(value, 0) / total) * 100);
}

export function squarify(values: number[], width: number, height: number): TreemapRect[] {
  const rects = values.map(() => ({ ...EMPTY_RECT }));
  const total = values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + Math.max(value, 0), 0);
  if (total <= 0 || width <= 0 || height <= 0) return rects;

  const scale = (width * height) / total;
  const items: WeightedItem[] = values
    .map((value, index) => ({ index, area: Math.max(value, 0) * scale }))
    .filter((item) => item.area > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.area - a.area);

  let frame = { x: 0, y: 0, width, height };
  let row: WeightedItem[] = [];
  let cursor = 0;

  while (cursor < items.length) {
    const item = items[cursor];
    if (!item) break;

    if (row.length === 0) {
      row.push(item);
      cursor += 1;
      continue;
    }

    const side = Math.min(frame.width, frame.height);
    const currentWorst = worstAspectRatio(
      row.map((entry) => entry.area),
      side,
    );
    const nextWorst = worstAspectRatio([...row.map((entry) => entry.area), item.area], side);

    if (nextWorst <= currentWorst) {
      row.push(item);
      cursor += 1;
      continue;
    }

    frame = placeRow(row, frame, rects);
    row = [];
  }

  if (row.length > 0) placeRow(row, frame, rects);

  return rects;
}

function worstAspectRatio(areas: number[], side: number): number {
  const rowArea = areas.reduce((sum, area) => sum + area, 0);
  if (rowArea <= 0 || side <= 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

  const thickness = rowArea / side;
  let worst = 1;

  for (const area of areas) {
    const length = area / thickness;
    if (length <= 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    worst = Math.max(worst, thickness / length, length / thickness);
  }

  return worst;
}

function placeRow(row: WeightedItem[], frame: TreemapRect, rects: TreemapRect[]): TreemapRect {
  const rowArea = row.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.area, 0);
  const horizontal = frame.width >= frame.height;
  const side = horizontal ? frame.height : frame.width;
  const thickness = rowArea / side;
  let offset = 0;

  for (const item of row) {
    const length = item.area / thickness;
    rects[item.index] = horizontal
      ? { x: frame.x, y: frame.y + offset, width: thickness, height: length }
      : { x: frame.x + offset, y: frame.y, width: length, height: thickness };
    offset += length;
  }

  return horizontal
    ? {
        x: frame.x + thickness,
        y: frame.y,
        width: Math.max(frame.width - thickness, 0),
        height: frame.height,
      }
    : {
        x: frame.x,
        y: frame.y + thickness,
        width: frame.width,
        height: Math.max(frame.height - thickness, 0),
      };
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/tile-treemap.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

import { cn } from "../lib/utils";
import { squarify, toPercentages } from "./tile-treemap.utils";

export type TileTreemapItem = {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  color?: string;
};

export type TileTreemapProps = {
  data: TileTreemapItem[];
  height?: number;
  className?: string;
  valueFormatter?: (value: number, percentage: number) => string;
  hoverScale?: number;
};

const CHART_COLORS = [
  "var(--chart-1)",
  "var(--chart-2)",
  "var(--chart-3)",
  "var(--chart-4)",
  "var(--chart-5)",
  "var(--chart-6)",
  "var(--chart-7)",
  "var(--chart-8)",
];

const DEFAULT_ASPECT_RATIO = "16 / 10";
const LABEL_MIN_WIDTH = 64;
const LABEL_MIN_HEIGHT = 40;
const PERCENT_MIN_WIDTH = 48;
const PERCENT_MIN_HEIGHT = 28;

export function TileTreemap({
  data,
  height,
  className,
  valueFormatter,
  hoverScale = 1.01,
}: TileTreemapProps) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [size, setSize] = useState({ width: 0, height: 0 });
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = containerRef.current;
    if (!element) return;

    const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      setSize((current) =>
        current.width === element.clientWidth && current.height === element.clientHeight
          ? current
          : { width: element.clientWidth, height: element.clientHeight },
      );
    });

    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const { rects, percentages } = useMemo(() => {
    const values = data.map((item) => item.value);
    return {
      rects: squarify(values, size.width, size.height),
      percentages: toPercentages(values),
    };
  }, [data, size.width, size.height]);

  if (data.length === 0 || !data.some((item) => item.value > 0)) {
    return (
      <div
        className={cn(
          "bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground flex min-h-40 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border text-sm",
          className,
        )}
      >
        No data to display.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className={cn("relative w-full", className)}
      style={height ? { height } : { aspectRatio: DEFAULT_ASPECT_RATIO }}
    >
      {size.width > 0 &&
        size.height > 0 &&
        data.map((item, index) => {
          const rect = rects[index];
          if (!rect || rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) return null;

          const percentage = percentages[index] ?? 0;
          const color = item.color ?? CHART_COLORS[index % CHART_COLORS.length];
          const formatted = valueFormatter
            ? valueFormatter(item.value, percentage)
            : `${percentage.toFixed(1)}%`;
          const showLabel = rect.width >= LABEL_MIN_WIDTH && rect.height >= LABEL_MIN_HEIGHT;
          const showPercentage =
            rect.width >= PERCENT_MIN_WIDTH && rect.height >= PERCENT_MIN_HEIGHT;

          return (
            <motion.div
              key={`${item.label}-${index}`}
              aria-label={`${item.label}: ${item.value} (${percentage.toFixed(1)}%)`}
              className="group absolute p-1 focus-visible:outline-none"
              role="img"
              style={{ left: rect.x, top: rect.y, width: rect.width, height: rect.height }}
              tabIndex={0}
              initial={reduceMotion ? false : { opacity: 0, scale: 0.92 }}
              animate={{
                opacity: 1,
                scale: 1,
                transition: {
                  duration: 0.3,
                  ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1],
                  delay: Math.min(index, 12) * 0.03,
                },
              }}
              whileHover={
                reduceMotion ? undefined : { scale: hoverScale, transition: { duration: 0.18 } }
              }
              whileFocus={
                reduceMotion ? undefined : { scale: hoverScale, transition: { duration: 0.18 } }
              }
            >
              <div
                className="group-focus-visible:ring-ring/60 flex size-full flex-col justify-end overflow-hidden rounded-lg p-2 transition-[filter] duration-150 ease-out group-hover:brightness-110 group-focus-visible:ring-2"
                style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
              >
                {showLabel && (
                  <span className="text-background truncate text-xs font-medium">{item.label}</span>
                )}
                {showPercentage && (
                  <span className="text-background/80 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums">
                    {formatted}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

# CLI Command

## packages/core/src/components/cli-command/package-managers.ts
```ts
export const PACKAGE_MANAGERS = ["npm", "pnpm", "yarn", "bun"] as const;

export type PackageManager = (typeof PACKAGE_MANAGERS)[number];

export function isPackageManager(value: string | null): value is PackageManager {
  return value !== null && (PACKAGE_MANAGERS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export type CliCommandSource =
  | { install: string; dev?: boolean }
  | { execute: string }
  | { run: string }
  | { commands: Record<PackageManager, string> };

const INSTALLERS: Record<PackageManager, string> = {
  npm: "npm install",
  pnpm: "pnpm add",
  yarn: "yarn add",
  bun: "bun add",
};

const EXECUTORS: Record<PackageManager, string> = {
  npm: "npx",
  pnpm: "pnpm dlx",
  yarn: "yarn dlx",
  bun: "bunx --bun",
};

const RUNNERS: Record<PackageManager, string> = {
  npm: "npm run",
  pnpm: "pnpm",
  yarn: "yarn",
  bun: "bun run",
};

function normalize(value: string): string {
  return value
    .split("\n")
    .map((line) => line.trim().replace(/[ \t]+/g, " "))
    .join("\n")
    .trim();
}

function withVerb(verb: string, body: string): string {
  return body === "" ? verb : `${verb} ${body}`;
}

function perManager(build: (manager: PackageManager) => string): Record<PackageManager, string> {
  return {
    npm: build("npm"),
    pnpm: build("pnpm"),
    yarn: build("yarn"),
    bun: build("bun"),
  };
}

export function resolveCommands(source: CliCommandSource): Record<PackageManager, string> {
  if ("commands" in source) {
    return { ...source.commands };
  }
  if ("install" in source) {
    const packages = normalize(source.install);
    const flag = source.dev === true ? " -D" : "";
    return perManager((manager) => withVerb(`${INSTALLERS[manager]}${flag}`, packages));
  }
  if ("execute" in source) {
    const command = normalize(source.execute);
    return perManager((manager) => withVerb(EXECUTORS[manager], command));
  }
  const script = normalize(source.run);
  return perManager((manager) => withVerb(RUNNERS[manager], script));
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/cli-command/shell-tokens.ts
```ts
export type ShellTokenType = "program" | "flag" | "string" | "operator" | "text" | "space";

export type ShellToken = {
  type: ShellTokenType;
  value: string;
};

const OPERATORS = new Set([";", "|"]);

function startsToken(line: string, index: number): boolean {
  const char = line[index];
  if (char === " " || char === "\t" || char === "'" || char === '"') {
    return true;
  }
  if (char !== undefined && OPERATORS.has(char)) {
    return true;
  }
  return char === "&" && line[index + 1] === "&";
}

/**
 * Splits one command line into colored spans. Concatenating every `value` gives the input back
 * byte for byte, so the rendered text never differs from the copied text.
 */
export function tokenizeCommand(line: string): ShellToken[] {
  const tokens: ShellToken[] = [];
  let index = 0;
  let nextIsProgram = true;

  while (index < line.length) {
    const char = line[index] as string;

    if (char === " " || char === "\t") {
      let value = "";
      while (index < line.length && (line[index] === " " || line[index] === "\t")) {
        value += line[index];
        index += 1;
      }
      tokens.push({ type: "space", value });
      continue;
    }

    if (char === "'" || char === '"') {
      let value = char;
      index += 1;
      while (index < line.length && line[index] !== char) {
        value += line[index];
        index += 1;
      }
      if (index < line.length) {
        value += line[index];
        index += 1;
      }
      tokens.push({ type: "string", value });
      nextIsProgram = false;
      continue;
    }

    if (OPERATORS.has(char) || (char === "&" && line[index + 1] === "&")) {
      let value = char;
      index += 1;
      if (index < line.length && line[index] === char) {
        value += line[index];
        index += 1;
      }
      tokens.push({ type: "operator", value });
      nextIsProgram = true;
      continue;
    }

    let value = "";
    while (index < line.length && !startsToken(line, index)) {
      value += line[index];
      index += 1;
    }
    const type: ShellTokenType = nextIsProgram
      ? "program"
      : value.startsWith("-")
        ? "flag"
        : "text";
    tokens.push({ type, value });
    nextIsProgram = false;
  }

  return tokens;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/cli-command/use-package-manager.ts
```ts
"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

import { isPackageManager, type PackageManager } from "./package-managers";

const STORAGE_KEY = "package-manager";
const CHANGE_EVENT = "package-manager-change";

function readStored(): PackageManager | null {
  try {
    const value = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    return isPackageManager(value) ? value : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function subscribe(onChange: () => void): () => void {
  window.addEventListener("storage", onChange);
  window.addEventListener(CHANGE_EVENT, onChange);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener("storage", onChange);
    window.removeEventListener(CHANGE_EVENT, onChange);
  };
}

/**
 * The selected package manager, shared by every `CliCommand` on the page and across browser tabs.
 * The server renders the default so the first paint matches the markup.
 */
export function usePackageManager(
  defaultManager: PackageManager,
): [PackageManager, (manager: PackageManager) => void] {
  const manager = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    () => readStored() ?? defaultManager,
    () => defaultManager,
  );

  const setManager = (next: PackageManager): void => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, next);
    } catch {
      // Storage can be blocked; the in-page event still updates every command.
    }
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CHANGE_EVENT));
  };

  return [manager, setManager];
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/cli-command/cli-command.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { Terminal } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "../../lib/utils";
import { CopyButton } from "../copy-button";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "../tabs";
import { PACKAGE_MANAGER_ICONS } from "./package-manager-icons";
import {
  isPackageManager,
  PACKAGE_MANAGERS,
  resolveCommands,
  type CliCommandSource,
  type PackageManager,
} from "./package-managers";
import { tokenizeCommand, type ShellTokenType } from "./shell-tokens";
import { usePackageManager } from "./use-package-manager";

export type CliCommandProps = (CliCommandSource | { command: string }) & {
  defaultManager?: PackageManager;
  prompt?: string;
  className?: string;
};

const DEFAULT_PROMPT = "$";

const TOKEN_CLASS: Record<ShellTokenType, string> = {
  program: "font-medium text-foreground",
  flag: "text-primary",
  string: "text-muted-foreground",
  operator: "text-muted-foreground",
  text: "text-foreground/80",
  space: "",
};

const CARD_CLASS = "@container bg-background overflow-hidden rounded-xl border";

// The active tab's `::after` must sit inside the trigger so `overflow-x-auto` on the list cannot
// clip it. The primitive positions it at `bottom-[-5px]`; `-bottom-px` lands it on the header
// border (the trigger's transparent 1px border offsets the absolutely positioned box).
// The list repeats the primitive's `group-data-horizontal/tabs:` variant on height and width so
// tailwind-merge drops the primitive's `h-8`/`w-fit` for one trigger-filled row.
const TAB_LIST_CLASS =
  "no-scrollbar group-data-horizontal/tabs:h-full w-full min-w-0 flex-1 justify-start gap-1 overflow-x-auto p-0 [&_[data-slot=tabs-trigger][data-state=active]]:after:-bottom-px";
const TRIGGER_CLASS = "h-full flex-none gap-1 [&>svg]:hidden @min-[20rem]:[&>svg]:block";

function CommandBody({ command, prompt }: { command: string; prompt: string }) {
  return (
    <pre className="overflow-x-auto p-4 font-mono text-sm whitespace-pre">
      <code>
        {command.split("\n").map((line, lineIndex) => (
          <span key={lineIndex} className="block">
            <span aria-hidden className="text-muted-foreground select-none">
              {prompt}{" "}
            </span>
            {tokenizeCommand(line).map((token, tokenIndex) => (
              <span key={tokenIndex} className={TOKEN_CLASS[token.type]}>
                {token.value}
              </span>
            ))}
          </span>
        ))}
      </code>
    </pre>
  );
}

function PlainCommand({
  command,
  prompt,
  className,
}: {
  command: string;
  prompt: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className={cn(CARD_CLASS, className)}>
      <header className="flex h-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b pr-2 pl-3">
        <Terminal className="text-muted-foreground size-4" />
        <CopyButton text={command} size="icon-sm" aria-label="Copy command" className="shrink-0" />
      </header>
      <CommandBody command={command} prompt={prompt} />
    </div>
  );
}

function PackageManagerCommand({
  source,
  defaultManager,
  prompt,
  className,
}: {
  source: CliCommandSource;
  defaultManager: PackageManager;
  prompt: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const [manager, setManager] = usePackageManager(defaultManager);
  const commands = resolveCommands(source);

  return (
    <Tabs
      value={manager}
      onValueChange={(value) => {
        if (isPackageManager(value)) setManager(value);
      }}
      className={cn(CARD_CLASS, "gap-0", className)}
    >
      <header className="flex h-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b pr-2 pl-1.5">
        <TabsList variant="line" className={TAB_LIST_CLASS}>
          {PACKAGE_MANAGERS.map((name) => {
            const Icon = PACKAGE_MANAGER_ICONS[name];
            return (
              <TabsTrigger key={name} value={name} className={TRIGGER_CLASS}>
                <Icon />
                {name}
              </TabsTrigger>
            );
          })}
        </TabsList>
        <CopyButton
          text={commands[manager]}
          size="icon-sm"
          aria-label="Copy command"
          className="shrink-0"
        />
      </header>
      {PACKAGE_MANAGERS.map((name) => (
        <TabsContent key={name} value={name}>
          <CommandBody command={commands[name]} prompt={prompt} />
        </TabsContent>
      ))}
    </Tabs>
  );
}

export function CliCommand({ prompt = DEFAULT_PROMPT, className, ...rest }: CliCommandProps) {
  if ("command" in rest) {
    return <PlainCommand command={rest.command} prompt={prompt} className={className} />;
  }
  return (
    <PackageManagerCommand
      source={rest}
      defaultManager={rest.defaultManager ?? "bun"}
      prompt={prompt}
      className={className}
    />
  );
}
```

# ASCII Ocean

> A canvas ocean of swimming animals: sharks and koi (fish, with vertical tails), and dolphins, orcas and humpbacks (mammals that surface to breathe and blow). Natural colours with a habitat water colour that shifts with depth, a water surface, and click splashes.

- Kind: component
- Docs: https://www.huanngdev.site/components/ascii-ocean
- Registry: https://www.huanngdev.site/r/ascii-ocean.json
- Apply: copy each file to the same path from the repository root. Leave the bytes unchanged.

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/math.ts
```ts
// Ported from https://github.com/stackedlol/ascii-sharks (used with the author's permission).
export const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

export const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);

/** Uniform random source, returning values in [0, 1). */
export type Rng = () => number;

/** Seeded 32-bit PRNG (mulberry32): the same seed always yields the same sequence. */
export function createRng(seed: number): Rng {
  let s = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    s = (s + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = s;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

export const rand = (rng: Rng, lo: number, hi: number) => lo + rng() * (hi - lo);

export const randInt = (rng: Rng, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.floor(rand(rng, lo, hi + 1));

export function smoothstep(e0: number, e1: number, x: number) {
  const t = clamp((x - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0, 1);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

/** Shortest signed angle from a to b, in (-PI, PI]. */
export function angleDiff(a: number, b: number) {
  let d = (b - a) % TAU;
  if (d > Math.PI) d -= TAU;
  if (d < -Math.PI) d += TAU;
  return d;
}

/** Cheap integer hash → [0, 1). Deterministic, so textures stay stable. */
export function hash3(x: number, y: number, z: number) {
  let h = Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y | 0, 668265263) ^ Math.imul(z | 0, 1442695041);
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);
  h ^= h >>> 16;
  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

const K1 = 1 / 260;
const K2 = 1 / 310;
const K3 = 1 / 190;
const CURRENT_SCALE = 3400;

/**
 * Divergence-free ocean current: the curl of a slowly drifting stream
 * function ψ(x, y, t). Because it has no sources or sinks, it moves the
 * schools around without bunching them up in one corner.
 */
export function current(x: number, y: number, t: number, out: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const a = x * K1 + t * 0.07;
  const b = y * K2 - t * 0.05;
  const c = (x + y) * K3 + t * 0.11;
  // u = ∂ψ/∂y, v = -∂ψ/∂x with ψ = sin(a)cos(b) + 0.5 sin(c)
  out.x = (-K2 * Math.sin(a) * Math.sin(b) + 0.5 * K3 * Math.cos(c)) * CURRENT_SCALE;
  out.y = (-K1 * Math.cos(a) * Math.cos(b) - 0.5 * K3 * Math.cos(c)) * CURRENT_SCALE;
  return out;
}

/** Converts OKLCH (L 0–1, C, H in degrees) to sRGB bytes 0–255, clamped to the gamut. */
export function oklchToRgb(l: number, c: number, h: number): [number, number, number] {
  const rad = (h * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a = c * Math.cos(rad);
  const b = c * Math.sin(rad);
  // OKLab → LMS (cube-rooted), then cube to LMS.
  const l_ = l + 0.3963377774 * a + 0.2158037573 * b;
  const m_ = l - 0.1055613458 * a - 0.0638541728 * b;
  const s_ = l - 0.0894841775 * a - 1.291485548 * b;
  const L3 = l_ * l_ * l_;
  const M3 = m_ * m_ * m_;
  const S3 = s_ * s_ * s_;
  // LMS → linear sRGB.
  const lin = [
    4.0767416621 * L3 - 3.3077115913 * M3 + 0.2309699292 * S3,
    -1.2684380046 * L3 + 2.6097574011 * M3 - 0.3413193965 * S3,
    -0.0041960863 * L3 - 0.7034186147 * M3 + 1.707614701 * S3,
  ];
  // Linear → gamma-encoded sRGB, then bytes.
  const encode = (x: number) =>
    Math.round(
      clamp((x <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * x : 1.055 * Math.pow(x, 1 / 2.4) - 0.055) * 255, 0, 255),
    );
  return [encode(lin[0]), encode(lin[1]), encode(lin[2])];
}

/** OKLab lightness (0–1) of an sRGB colour given as bytes 0–255: the inverse of the lightness path in oklchToRgb. */
export function rgbToOklabL(r: number, g: number, b: number): number {
  const lin = (c: number) => {
    const x = c / 255;
    return x <= 0.04045 ? x / 12.92 : Math.pow((x + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
  };
  const R = lin(r);
  const G = lin(g);
  const B = lin(b);
  // Linear sRGB → LMS, cube root, then the lightness row of the OKLab matrix.
  const l = Math.cbrt(0.4122214708 * R + 0.5363325363 * G + 0.0514459929 * B);
  const m = Math.cbrt(0.2119034982 * R + 0.6806995451 * G + 0.1073969566 * B);
  const s = Math.cbrt(0.0883024619 * R + 0.2817188376 * G + 0.6299787005 * B);
  return 0.2104542553 * l + 0.793617785 * m - 0.0040720468 * s;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/palette.ts
```ts
import { DEFAULT_PALETTE, type OceanPalette } from "./renderer";

/**
 * Returns a reader that takes the page colours from an element's `background-color` and `color`.
 * It keeps one probe canvas, created on first use, to resolve any CSS colour (including oklch()).
 */
export function createPaletteReader(): (el: HTMLElement) => OceanPalette {
  let probe: CanvasRenderingContext2D | null = null;

  /** Resolves any CSS colour (including oklch()) to rgba bytes via a reusable 1×1 canvas. */
  const toRgba = (css: string): Uint8ClampedArray | null => {
    if (!probe) {
      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.width = 1;
      canvas.height = 1;
      probe = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
    }
    const ctx = probe;
    if (!ctx) return null;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#000";
    ctx.fillStyle = css;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
    return ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
  };

  return (el: HTMLElement): OceanPalette => {
    const style = getComputedStyle(el);
    const bg = toRgba(style.backgroundColor);
    const fg = toRgba(style.color);
    // No background colour (transparent): keep the default look.
    if (!bg || !fg || bg[3] === 0) return DEFAULT_PALETTE;
    const luminance = (0.2126 * bg[0] + 0.7152 * bg[1] + 0.0722 * bg[2]) / 255;
    return {
      background: [bg[0], bg[1], bg[2]],
      foreground: [fg[0], fg[1], fg[2]],
      mode: luminance > 0.5 ? "light" : "dark",
    };
  };
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/species.ts
```ts
// Ported from https://github.com/stackedlol/ascii-sharks (used with the author's permission).
import type { Rng } from "./math";

export interface Species {
  name: string;
  cruise: number;
  maxSpeed: number;
  minSpeed: number;
  /** Relative odds of a new school being this species. */
  weight: number;
  schoolSize: [number, number];
  /** Personal space in grid cells (x, y), so every fish gets its own glyph. */
  spacing: [number, number];
  // Behaviour weights. Different mixes produce different collective shapes
  // (Couzin et al. 2002): strong alignment gives polarised schools and
  // ribbons, low alignment gives swarms, and a tangential pull gives a mill.
  align: number;
  cohesion: number;
  jitter: number;
  /** Tangential pull around the local centre: >0 makes a rotating bait ball. */
  mill: number;
  /** Damps vertical speed; real fish mostly cruise level. */
  verticalDrag: number;
}

export const SPECIES: Species[] = [
  {
    name: "sardine",
    cruise: 36,
    maxSpeed: 64,
    minSpeed: 18,
    weight: 3,
    schoolSize: [18, 28],
    spacing: [1.8, 1.1],
    align: 1.6,
    cohesion: 1.6,
    jitter: 40,
    mill: 70,
    verticalDrag: 0.5,
  },
  {
    name: "anchovy",
    cruise: 46,
    maxSpeed: 78,
    minSpeed: 22,
    weight: 3,
    schoolSize: [20, 32],
    spacing: [1.5, 1],
    align: 3.2,
    cohesion: 0.45,
    jitter: 30,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 1.4,
  },
  {
    name: "silverside",
    cruise: 40,
    maxSpeed: 70,
    minSpeed: 20,
    weight: 3,
    schoolSize: [12, 20],
    spacing: [2, 1.1],
    align: 2.4,
    cohesion: 1.1,
    jitter: 35,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 1.6,
  },
  {
    name: "mackerel",
    cruise: 32,
    maxSpeed: 60,
    minSpeed: 16,
    weight: 2,
    schoolSize: [5, 9],
    spacing: [2.6, 1.3],
    align: 2,
    cohesion: 0.9,
    jitter: 25,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 2,
  },
  {
    name: "goby",
    cruise: 26,
    maxSpeed: 56,
    minSpeed: 10,
    weight: 1,
    schoolSize: [8, 12],
    spacing: [1.4, 1.1],
    align: 0.35,
    cohesion: 1.4,
    jitter: 110,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 0.3,
  },
];

const TOTAL_WEIGHT = SPECIES.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.weight, 0);

export function pickSpecies(rng: Rng) {
  let r = rng() * TOTAL_WEIGHT;
  for (let i = 0; i < SPECIES.length; i++) {
    r -= SPECIES[i].weight;
    if (r <= 0) return i;
  }
  return 0;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/types.ts
```ts
import type { SharkKind } from "../world";
import type { TaxonId } from "./taxa";

/** Every animal the sim and renderer know how to draw. Later rounds extend this union. */
export type CreatureSpecies = SharkKind | "koi" | "bottlenose" | "orca" | "humpback";

/** Which animals to put in the water. `size` is an extra multiplier on the real-size scaling. */
export type CreatureGroup = {
  species: CreatureSpecies;
  /** How many of this species, 1–12. Default 1. */
  count?: number;
  /** Extra size multiplier on top of the species' real-size scaling. Default 1. */
  size?: number;
  /** Coat to use, or a list to pick from per animal (koi: kohaku, sanke, showa, tancho, ogon, asagi). Ignored by species without coats. */
  variety?: string | string[];
};

/** `ocean` is the open sea; `pond` is a bait-free tank, so baitFish defaults to off. */
export type OceanScene = "ocean" | "pond";

/** `stylized` compresses the size gap between species; `true` keeps real length ratios. */
export type OceanScale = "stylized" | "true";

/** What a pointer press does to the water. */
export type OceanInteraction =
  /** A press flashes the water. */
  | "splash"
  /** Presses are ignored. */
  | "none";

/** OKLCH colour: lightness 0–1, chroma, hue in degrees. */
export type Oklch = [number, number, number];

/** Koi colour varieties; each one is a coat on the koi species. */
export type KoiVariety = "kohaku" | "sanke" | "showa" | "tancho" | "ogon" | "asagi";

/** Where on the body a point is, for painting: s along (0 snout → 1 tail), v across in px (signed), L the body length, across 0 (spine) → 1 (edge of the body), flag the renderer's cell flag (body, fin, ridge, eye...). */
export type PaintPoint = {
  s: number;
  v: number;
  L: number;
  across: number;
  flag: number;
  id: number;
};

/** Natural colours: `regions[0]` is the base skin; `paint` returns a region index for a point. */
export interface Coat {
  regions: readonly Oklch[];
  paint?: (p: PaintPoint) => number;
  /** Sparkle the scale glyphs in pale gold and white instead of the accent colours (ogon). */
  glint?: boolean;
  /** Shade the glyphs on light regions with a see-through dark, so they tint the skin's colour instead of dirtying it (koi). */
  softInk?: boolean;
}

/** `natural` paints animals in their real colours on any theme; `theme` keeps them greyscale. */
export type OceanColors = "natural" | "theme";

/** The tail fin, drawn in place of the tail body. */
export type TailShape =
  /** Whale/dolphin flukes: a horizontal, swept-back wing. From `1 - length` to the tail tip; the tips reach the end, the centre notch sits `notch` before it. */
  | { kind: "fluke"; span: number; length: number; notch: number }
  /** A fish's vertical caudal fin seen from above: a narrow blade behind the peduncle. `width` is its half-width at the tip at rest, `twist` how far the tip sways to each side at the peak of a beat, both fractions of length. */
  | { kind: "caudal"; length: number; width: number; twist: number }
  /**
   * A shark's heterocercal tail: a vertical caudal fin seen edge-on from above, so a narrow blade.
   * It starts `length` (a fraction of the body length) behind the body tip, and its upper lobe runs
   * `upper` (a fraction of the total length) past the tip. The body is shortened so that snout to
   * blade tip is the full length. `width` is the half-width at rest and `twist` how far the tip sways
   * to each side at the peak of a beat, both fractions of the total length.
   */
  | { kind: "heterocercal"; length: number; upper: number; width: number; twist: number };

/** Body outline and fin layout per species. All values are fractions of length. */
export interface BodyShape {
  halfWidth: (s: number) => number;
  /** Pectoral fin: leading root, trailing root, tip position along the body, and reach. */
  pectoral: [number, number, number, number];
  /** Notched (falcate) trailing edge on the pectorals. */
  falcate: boolean;
  /** Rounded paddle pectorals (koi, orca) instead of the triangle; `falcate` is ignored. */
  paddle?: boolean;
  /** Pelvic fins: leading root, trailing root, tip, reach. */
  pelvic: [number, number, number, number];
  dorsal: [number, number];
  secondDorsal: [number, number];
  /**
   * Hammerheads' cephalofoil: half-span, how far its tips sweep back, its depth, and the
   * notch in the middle of the front edge. All fractions of length; absent for other sharks.
   */
  head?: { span: number; sweep: number; depth: number; notch: number };
  /** null when the eyes sit somewhere else (hammerhead: on the cephalofoil tips). */
  eye: [number, number] | null;
  gills: [number, number];
  /** Skin colour multipliers (r, g, b). */
  tint: [number, number, number];
  /** The tail fin: a fluke, a koi's caudal blade, or a shark's heterocercal blade. */
  tail?: TailShape;
  /** Multiplies the side-to-side body wave. Default: the taxon's `undulation`. Whales and dolphins beat their tails up and down, so from above they barely bend. */
  undulation?: number;
  /** Pectoral flex amplitude. Default 0.06. */
  finFlap?: number;
  /** Pectoral flex frequency as a multiple of the tail-beat phase. Default 0.5. */
  finFlapRate?: number;
}

export interface CreatureDef {
  /** Typical adult length in metres. Sets the size ratios between species. */
  realLength: number;
  /** Fish or mammal; the taxon supplies the traits this species does not set itself. */
  taxon: TaxonId;
  shape: BodyShape;
  /** Dark skin markings at body point (s along, v across); `id` seeds per-animal patterns. */
  marking?: (s: number, v: number, L: number, across: number, id: number) => boolean;
  /** Pale ridges along the back (zebra shark). */
  paleRidge?: (s: number, across: number) => boolean;
  /** Named natural colour schemes (koi varieties). Sharks leave it out and keep their tinted greys. */
  coats?: Record<string, Coat>;
  /** Coat used when a group gives no variety. */
  defaultCoat?: string;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/paint.ts
```ts
import { hash3 } from "../math";
import type { Coat, Oklch } from "./types";

// The renderer's fin flag, repeated here for the same reason as in the species files.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

/**
 * Smooth value noise in [0, 1): bilinear blend of hashed lattice values, eased with smoothstep
 * so the pattern has no visible grid. Deterministic for a given (x, y, seed).
 */
export function valueNoise(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
  const x0 = Math.floor(x);
  const y0 = Math.floor(y);
  const tx = x - x0;
  const ty = y - y0;
  const u = tx * tx * (3 - 2 * tx);
  const v = ty * ty * (3 - 2 * ty);
  const a = hash3(x0, y0, seed);
  const b = hash3(x0 + 1, y0, seed);
  const c = hash3(x0, y0 + 1, seed);
  const d = hash3(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, seed);
  return a + (b - a) * u + (c - a) * v + (a - b - c + d) * u * v;
}

/**
 * Body-anchored patch noise: `s` along the body, `vOverL` across it as a fraction of the length,
 * `scale` the patch size, and `salt` picks an independent pattern. Patches ride along with the skin.
 */
export function blobs(s: number, vOverL: number, id: number, scale: number, salt: number): number {
  return valueNoise(s / scale, vOverL / scale, id * 131 + salt);
}

/**
 * Countershading, seen from above: `back` over the top, `belly` past `edge` (0 at the spine, 1 at the
 * flank edge). The belly shows only as a thin rim, with a sharp line where it meets the back. Fins take the back.
 */
export function countershaded(back: Oklch, belly: Oklch, edge = 0.8): Coat {
  return {
    regions: [back, belly],
    paint: (p) => (p.flag !== FLAG_FIN && p.across > edge ? 1 : 0),
  };
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/sharks.ts
```ts
import { hash3, smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { SharkKind } from "../world";
import { countershaded } from "./paint";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's fin flag, repeated here because importing the renderer would make a cycle.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

/** Classic top-down half-width at s ∈ [0, 1] (snout → tail), as a fraction of length. */
function classicHalfWidth(s: number) {
  // anatomy: [est. 1] conical snout tapering to a point, then full width ~0.18 TL at the gills.
  if (s < 0.16) return 0.1 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.16) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.7);
  if (s < 0.38) return 0.1 + 0.005 * smoothstep(0.16, 0.38, s);
  if (s < 0.84) return 0.019 + 0.086 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.38) / 0.46, 1.25);
  const k = (s - 0.84) / 0.16;
  return 0.019 * (1 - k) + 0.012 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
}

/** Tiger shark bars: wavy vertical bars over the back half, fading out toward the head. */
function tigerBar(s: number, v: number, L: number, across: number) {
  if (s < 0.2 || s > 0.9 || across > 0.85) return false;
  const wobble = 0.35 * Math.sin((v / L) * 25 + s * 5);
  const bar = Math.sin(s * Math.PI * 2 * 9 + wobble);
  return bar > 0.65 - 0.5 * smoothstep(0.2, 0.42, s) + 0.4 * smoothstep(0.8, 0.9, s);
}

/** Leopard spots on a jittered grid, so they ride along with the skin. */
function zebraSpot(s: number, v: number, L: number, id: number) {
  if (s < 0.07) return false;
  const gu = (s * L) / (L * 0.06);
  const gv = v / (L * 0.045);
  const cu = Math.floor(gu);
  const cv = Math.floor(gv);
  const jx = hash3(cu, cv, id) * 0.5 + 0.25;
  const jy = hash3(cu, cv, id + 11) * 0.5 + 0.25;
  return (gu - cu - jx) ** 2 + (gv - cv - jy) ** 2 < 0.08;
}

/** Pale ridges down the back, between the spots. */
function zebraRidge(s: number, across: number) {
  return s > 0.08 && s < 0.55 && (across < 0.1 || Math.abs(across - 0.6) < 0.08);
}

// Natural colours, OKLCH. Dorsal view: the back and flank show, the belly only as a rim (see countershaded).
// anatomy: [est. 1] great white: dark grey to blue-grey back, white belly.
const CLASSIC_BACK: Oklch = [0.42, 0.02, 250];
const CLASSIC_BELLY: Oklch = [0.96, 0.005, 250];
// anatomy: [est. 1] scalloped hammerhead: olive-grey back, paler belly.
const HAMMER_BACK: Oklch = [0.5, 0.035, 110];
const HAMMER_BELLY: Oklch = [0.9, 0.015, 95];
// anatomy: [est. 1] great hammerhead: slate-grey back, white belly.
const GREATHAMMER_BACK: Oklch = [0.46, 0.025, 250];
const GREATHAMMER_BELLY: Oklch = [0.95, 0.005, 250];
// anatomy: [est. 1] tiger: grey-brown back, darker bars, pale belly.
const TIGER_BACK: Oklch = [0.5, 0.035, 70];
const TIGER_BAR: Oklch = [0.27, 0.025, 60];
const TIGER_BELLY: Oklch = [0.9, 0.02, 85];
// anatomy: [est. 1] zebra: tan to yellow-brown back, dark spots, pale ridges.
const ZEBRA_BACK: Oklch = [0.72, 0.08, 85];
const ZEBRA_SPOT: Oklch = [0.3, 0.04, 55];
const ZEBRA_RIDGE: Oklch = [0.85, 0.05, 90];
const ZEBRA_BELLY: Oklch = [0.9, 0.04, 90];
// anatomy: [est. 1] shortfin mako: metallic indigo-blue back, white belly.
const MAKO_BACK: Oklch = [0.38, 0.1, 275];
const MAKO_BELLY: Oklch = [0.95, 0.005, 250];
// anatomy: [est. 1] thresher: bronze to purplish-grey back, white belly.
const THRESHER_BACK: Oklch = [0.45, 0.06, 310];
const THRESHER_BELLY: Oklch = [0.95, 0.01, 90];
// anatomy: [est. 1] nurse: uniform tawny yellow-brown, pale belly.
const NURSE_BACK: Oklch = [0.6, 0.08, 70];
const NURSE_BELLY: Oklch = [0.9, 0.03, 85];

const tigerCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [TIGER_BACK, TIGER_BAR, TIGER_BELLY],
  paint: (p: PaintPoint) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
    if (p.across > 0.8) return 2;
    return tigerBar(p.s, p.v, p.L, p.across) ? 1 : 0;
  },
};

const zebraCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [ZEBRA_BACK, ZEBRA_SPOT, ZEBRA_RIDGE, ZEBRA_BELLY],
  paint: (p: PaintPoint) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
    if (p.across > 0.8) return 3;
    if (zebraSpot(p.s, p.v, p.L, p.id)) return 1;
    return zebraRidge(p.s, p.across) ? 2 : 0;
  },
};

/**
 * Shark body outlines, fin layouts and skin, per species. Tail blades (heterocercal) use the values in
 * docs/ascii-ocean-anatomy.md; where the research marked a length as an estimate, the value is one too.
 * Each shark has a natural coat; theme mode ignores coats and keeps the greyscale silhouette.
 */
export const SHARKS: Record<SharkKind, CreatureDef> = {
  classic: {
    realLength: 5,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: classicHalfWidth,
      // anatomy: [est. 1] root 0.16–0.2 TL, length ~0.2 TL, falcate.
      pectoral: [0.16, 0.3, 0.46, 0.32],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.57, 0.65, 0.69, 0.085],
      // anatomy: [est. 1] first dorsal origin 0.3–0.35 TL, base ~0.1 TL.
      dorsal: [0.32, 0.42],
      secondDorsal: [0.71, 0.76],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.22, upper: 0.05, width: 0.012, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: [0.1, 0.14],
      gills: [0.19, 0.29],
      tint: [0.92, 0.97, 1.05],
    },
    coats: { classic: countershaded(CLASSIC_BACK, CLASSIC_BELLY) },
    defaultCoat: "classic",
  },
  // Slim neck under a wide, swept-back cephalofoil (drawn in sampleBody),
  // tall first dorsal, long upper tail lobe. Bronze-olive grey.
  hammerhead: {
    realLength: 3.5,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.06) return 0.045;
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.045 + 0.04 * smoothstep(0.06, 0.3, s);
        return classicHalfWidth(s) * 0.81;
      },
      // anatomy: [est. 1] full head width ~0.24–0.3 TL (span is the half-width, so 0.135).
      head: { span: 0.135, sweep: 0.035, depth: 0.09, notch: 0 },
      // anatomy: [est. 1] root ~0.18 TL, length ~0.2 TL, falcate.
      pectoral: [0.18, 0.31, 0.44, 0.3],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.56, 0.63, 0.67, 0.075],
      // anatomy: [est. 1] first dorsal origin ~0.28 TL.
      dorsal: [0.28, 0.38],
      secondDorsal: [0.7, 0.74],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.2, upper: 0.05, width: 0.012, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: null,
      gills: [0.16, 0.26],
      tint: [0.96, 0.98, 0.9],
    },
    coats: { hammer: countershaded(HAMMER_BACK, HAMMER_BELLY) },
    defaultCoat: "hammer",
  },
  // Blunt, square snout on a heavy body; barred back. Warm grey-brown.
  tiger: {
    realLength: 3.8,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        // anatomy: [est. 1] square, blunt snout; full width ~0.18 TL at the gills.
        if (s < 0.12) return 0.1 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.12) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.3);
        if (s < 0.34) return 0.1 + 0.005 * smoothstep(0.12, 0.34, s);
        if (s < 0.84) return 0.019 + 0.086 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.34) / 0.5, 1.2);
        const k = (s - 0.84) / 0.16;
        return 0.019 * (1 - k) + 0.012 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      // anatomy: [5] broad falcate triangle: root ~0.14 TL, reach ~0.3 TL past the edge, tip swept back.
      pectoral: [0.16, 0.3, 0.46, 0.3],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.57, 0.65, 0.69, 0.085],
      dorsal: [0.33, 0.44],
      secondDorsal: [0.71, 0.75],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.3, upper: 0.04, width: 0.012, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: [0.08, 0.12],
      gills: [0.19, 0.28],
      tint: [1.02, 0.95, 0.84],
    },
    marking: (s, v, L, across) => tigerBar(s, v, L, across),
    coats: { tiger: tigerCoat },
    defaultCoat: "tiger",
  },
  // Short rounded head, body done by half-length, then a long ribbon of a
  // tail fin. Broad rounded pectorals, spotted sandy skin.
  zebra: {
    realLength: 2.3,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.1) return 0.08 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.1) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.7);
        if (s < 0.28) return 0.075 + 0.005 * smoothstep(0.1, 0.28, s);
        if (s < 0.55) return 0.022 + 0.058 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.28) / 0.27, 1.1);
        const k = (s - 0.55) / 0.45;
        return 0.022 * (1 - k * k) + 0.012 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      // anatomy: [est. 1] broad, rounded tips: root ~0.12 TL, length ~0.2 TL.
      pectoral: [0.12, 0.3, 0.36, 0.26],
      falcate: false,
      pelvic: [0.42, 0.48, 0.51, 0.065],
      dorsal: [0.36, 0.44],
      secondDorsal: [0.49, 0.53],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.35, upper: 0.03, width: 0.012, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: [0.06, 0.09],
      gills: [0.13, 0.2],
      tint: [1.08, 0.97, 0.74],
    },
    marking: (s, v, L, _across, id) => zebraSpot(s, v, L, id),
    // Pale ridges down the back, between the spots.
    paleRidge: zebraRidge,
    coats: { zebra: zebraCoat },
    defaultCoat: "zebra",

  // Great hammerhead: a broader, nearly straight-edged cephalofoil with a notch at the centre,
  // a towering sickle first dorsal (drawn long and bright from above), and a cooler slate skin.
  greathammer: {
    realLength: 5,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.05) return 0.04;
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.04 + 0.045 * smoothstep(0.05, 0.3, s);
        return classicHalfWidth(s) * 0.81;

      // anatomy: [3][4] full head width 23–27% TL, so the half-span is 0.125.
      head: { span: 0.125, sweep: 0.006, depth: 0.085, notch: 0.014 },
      // anatomy: [est. 1] root ~0.16 TL, length ~0.2 TL, falcate.
      pectoral: [0.16, 0.32, 0.46, 0.3],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.55, 0.62, 0.67, 0.085],
      // anatomy: [est. 1] first dorsal origin ~0.24 TL.
      dorsal: [0.24, 0.45],
      secondDorsal: [0.68, 0.74],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.22, upper: 0.05, width: 0.012, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: null,
      gills: [0.15, 0.25],
      tint: [0.86, 0.93, 1.02],
    },
    coats: { greathammer: countershaded(GREATHAMMER_BACK, GREATHAMMER_BELLY) },
    defaultCoat: "greathammer",

  // Shortfin mako: a long conical snout, a torpedo body that narrows hard to the tail stalk,
  // and a tall crescent tail. Metallic indigo back.
  mako: {
    realLength: 3.5,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        // anatomy: [6] long conical snout: the front tapers almost linearly to the full width.
        if (s < 0.2) return 0.09 * Math.sin(((s / 0.2) * Math.PI) / 2);
        if (s < 0.32) return 0.09;
        if (s < 0.86) return 0.014 + 0.076 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.32) / 0.54, 1.45);
        const k = (s - 0.86) / 0.14;

      falcate: true,

      dorsal: [0.34, 0.45],
      secondDorsal: [0.76, 0.79],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.22, upper: 0.06, width: 0.012, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: [0.11, 0.15],
      gills: [0.2, 0.3],
      tint: [0.78, 0.9, 1.2],
    },
    coats: { mako: countershaded(MAKO_BACK, MAKO_BELLY) },
    defaultCoat: "mako",
  },
  // Thresher: a compact body over the front half, then the upper tail lobe as a long whip
  // that's as long as the shark itself. Big eyes, long sickle pectorals, bronze-violet skin.
  thresher: {
    realLength: 5,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.08) return 0.07 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.08) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.75);
        if (s < 0.2) return 0.07 + 0.02 * smoothstep(0.08, 0.2, s);
        if (s < 0.48) return 0.012 + 0.078 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.2) / 0.28, 1.3);
        // The whip: thin, with a slight sickle swell before it tapers out.
        const k = (s - 0.48) / 0.52;
        return 0.012 * (1 - k) + 0.006 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI * 0.9) + 0.002;
      },
      // anatomy: [est. 1] long and sickle-shaped: root ~0.12 TL, length 0.2–0.25 TL.
      pectoral: [0.12, 0.27, 0.36, 0.3],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.37, 0.41, 0.44, 0.045],
      dorsal: [0.24, 0.32],
      secondDorsal: [0.45, 0.47],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.5, upper: 0.45, width: 0.01, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: [0.05, 0.1],
      gills: [0.11, 0.18],
      tint: [1.0, 0.9, 1.08],
    },
    coats: { thresher: countershaded(THRESHER_BACK, THRESHER_BELLY) },
    defaultCoat: "thresher",
  },
  // Nurse shark: a blunt, broad head, a heavy flattened body with both dorsals set far back,
  // rounded paddle pectorals, and a long low tail with no lower lobe. Tawny brown.
  nurse: {
    realLength: 2.8,
    taxon: "fish",
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.08) return 0.085 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.08) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.4);
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.085 + 0.025 * smoothstep(0.08, 0.3, s);
        if (s < 0.72) return 0.022 + 0.088 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.3) / 0.42, 1.1);
        const k = (s - 0.72) / 0.28;
        return 0.022 * (1 - k * k) + 0.014 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      // anatomy: [11] broad, rounded tips: root ~0.16 TL, length ~0.17 TL. Drawn as paddles.
      pectoral: [0.16, 0.3, 0.36, 0.22],
      paddle: true,
      falcate: false,
      pelvic: [0.45, 0.52, 0.55, 0.075],
      dorsal: [0.5, 0.58],
      secondDorsal: [0.63, 0.69],
      tail: { kind: "heterocercal", length: 0.27, upper: 0.04, width: 0.02, twist: 0.05 },
      eye: [0.05, 0.075],
      gills: [0.14, 0.22],
      tint: [1.1, 0.96, 0.76],
    },
    coats: { nurse: countershaded(NURSE_BACK, NURSE_BELLY) },
    defaultCoat: "nurse",
  },
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/koi.ts
```ts
import { smoothstep } from "../math";
import { blobs } from "./paint";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, KoiVariety, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const SHIROJI: Oklch = [0.985, 0.012, 90];
const HI: Oklch = [0.66, 0.21, 35];
const SUMI: Oklch = [0.22, 0.01, 260];
const YAMABUKI: Oklch = [0.8, 0.15, 85];
const ASAGI: Oklch = [0.6, 0.045, 240];
const ASAGI_NET: Oklch = [0.74, 0.03, 235];

const fract = (x: number) => x - Math.floor(x);

/** Kohaku's red patches on white; a red cap on the head for two in three fish. Shared with sanke. */
function kohakuRed(p: PaintPoint) {
  if (p.s < 0.03 || p.s > 0.86) return false;
  const cap = p.s < 0.14 && p.id % 3 !== 0;
  return blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.09, 1) > (cap ? 0.35 : 0.5);
}

/** Realistic koi colourings, from the species notes. Region 0 is always the base skin. */
const coats: Record<KoiVariety, Coat> = {
  // White with red patches; white fins.
  kohaku: {
    regions: [SHIROJI, HI],
    softInk: true,
    paint: (p) => (p.flag !== FLAG_FIN && kohakuRed(p) ? 1 : 0),
  },
  // Kohaku with small black spots on the white, none on the head.
  sanke: {
    regions: [SHIROJI, HI, SUMI],
    softInk: true,
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
      if (kohakuRed(p)) return 1;
      if (
        p.s >= 0.15 &&
        p.s <= 0.8 &&
        p.across < 0.85 &&
        blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.035, 7) > 0.8
      ) {
        return 2;
      }
      return 0;
    },
  },
  // Black base with red and white over it; red wins where they overlap. Black reaches the head.
  // Pectoral roots (motoguro) are black; the other fins are white.
  showa: {
    regions: [SUMI, HI, SHIROJI],
    softInk: true,
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return p.s < 0.4 && p.across < 0.35 ? 0 : 2;
      // Thresholds tuned so black stays the commonest colour (the noise centres on 0.5).
      if (blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.1, 3) > 0.62) return 1;
      if (blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.08, 5) > (p.s < 0.12 ? 0.8 : 0.66)) return 2;
      return 0;
    },
  },
  // White with one round red spot on the head, centred at 0.075 L and radius 0.045 L.
  tancho: {
    regions: [SHIROJI, HI],
    softInk: true,
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
      const dx = (p.s - 0.075) * p.L;
      const r = 0.045 * p.L;
      return dx * dx + p.v * p.v < r * r ? 1 : 0;
    },
  },
  // A single metallic gold colour that catches the light.
  ogon: {
    regions: [YAMABUKI],
    softInk: true,
    glint: true,
    paint: () => 0,
  },
  // Blue-grey back with a lighter scale net; red flanks and pectoral roots; a pale plain head.
  asagi: {
    regions: [ASAGI, ASAGI_NET, HI],
    softInk: true,
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return p.s < 0.4 && p.across < 0.35 ? 2 : 1;
      if (p.s < 0.14) return 1;
      if (p.across > 0.62) return 2;
      // Scale net: lines on a grid of cells 0.035 L square.
      return fract(p.s / 0.035) < 0.2 || fract(p.v / p.L / 0.035) < 0.2 ? 1 : 0;
    },
  },
};

/** Thick, deep-bodied spindle with a blunt head, large round pectorals that scull, and a long low dorsal. */
export const KOI: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 0.75,
  taxon: "fish",
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Blunt head: fills out fast, then holds its depth through the shoulders.
      if (s < 0.12) return 0.11 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.12) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.5);
      if (s < 0.42) return 0.11 + 0.01 * smoothstep(0.12, 0.42, s);
      // One continuous taper from the shoulders to a short peduncle, then a thick stalk into the fin.
      if (s < 0.76) return 0.12 - 0.07 * smoothstep(0.42, 0.76, s);
      if (s < 0.82) return 0.05 - 0.01 * smoothstep(0.76, 0.82, s);
      return 0.04 - 0.005 * smoothstep(0.82, 1, s);
    },
    // Large round paddles just behind the head: the tip sits behind the leading root, so they fan sideways.
    pectoral: [0.12, 0.26, 0.36, 0.25],
    paddle: true,
    falcate: false,
    pelvic: [0.44, 0.52, 0.6, 0.12],
    dorsal: [0.3, 0.62],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.06, 0.09],
    gills: [0.13, 0.17],
    tint: [1, 1, 1],
    tail: { kind: "caudal", length: 0.24, width: 0.07, twist: 0.06 },
    undulation: 0.55,
    finFlap: 0.25,
    finFlapRate: 1.4,
  },
  coats,
  defaultCoat: "kohaku",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/bottlenose.ts
```ts
import { smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const CAPE: Oklch = [0.42, 0.02, 245];
const FLANK: Oklch = [0.62, 0.015, 240];
const BEAK: Oklch = [0.52, 0.018, 245];
// Flippers are a shade darker than the cape, so they read against it; the leading edge is lighter, like the flank.
const FLIPPER: Oklch = [0.3, 0.02, 245];
const FLIPPER_EDGE: Oklch = [0.74, 0.012, 240];

/**
 * Dark grey cape over the back, lighter flanks, and a darker beak. Flippers are a darker slate with a lighter
 * leading edge, so they stay visible against the cape and the flank.
 */
const bottlenoseCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [CAPE, FLANK, BEAK, FLIPPER, FLIPPER_EDGE],
  paint: (p: PaintPoint) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return p.s < 0.17 ? 4 : 3;
    if (p.s < 0.06) return 2;
    // The cape narrows behind the dorsal, down to across 0.35 toward the tail stock.
    const cape = 0.5 - 0.15 * smoothstep(0.52, 0.7, p.s);
    return p.across < cape ? 0 : 1;
  },
};

/** Bottlenose dolphin: a short beak, a round melon, a long barely-bending body, pointed flippers, and wide flukes. */
export const BOTTLENOSE: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 3,
  taxon: "mammal",
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Short beak, then the melon swells up behind it.
      // anatomy: [est. 1] short beak, round melon; full width ~0.2 TL.
      if (s < 0.04) return 0.025;
      if (s < 0.12) return 0.025 + 0.06 * smoothstep(0.04, 0.12, s);
      if (s < 0.3) return 0.085 + 0.015 * smoothstep(0.12, 0.3, s);
      // Tapers into a narrow tail stock.
      if (s < 0.86) return 0.1 - 0.08 * Math.pow((s - 0.3) / 0.56, 1.3);
      return 0.02 - 0.005 * smoothstep(0.86, 1, s);
    },
    // anatomy: [est. 1] falcate flipper, root just behind the eye (~0.14 TL), reach ~0.25 TL past the spine.
    // Stylised: reach 0.30 rather than 0.25 so the flipper clears the body by 3+ cells at the species-tab size.
    pectoral: [0.14, 0.22, 0.3, 0.3],
    falcate: true,
    pelvic: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    dorsal: [0.42, 0.52],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.1, 0.12],
    gills: [0, 0],
    tint: [0.95, 0.98, 1.04],
    // anatomy: [est. 1] fluke span ~0.2 TL (half-span 0.11).
    tail: { kind: "fluke", span: 0.11, length: 0.18, notch: 0.03 },
    undulation: 0.15,
    finFlap: 0.03,
  },
  coats: { bottlenose: bottlenoseCoat },
  defaultCoat: "bottlenose",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/orca.ts
```ts
import { smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const BLACK: Oklch = [0.17, 0.01, 260];
const WHITE: Oklch = [0.96, 0.005, 250];
const SADDLE_GREY: Oklch = [0.6, 0.01, 250];
// The flipper is black like the body, so its tip rim is a lighter grey to outline it.
const FIN_RIM: Oklch = [0.5, 0.01, 260];

/**
 * Killer whale, black base. A white eye patch sits behind and above each eye, and a grey saddle
 * patch sits just behind the dorsal fin. Fins are black.
 */
const orcaCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [BLACK, WHITE, SADDLE_GREY, FIN_RIM],
  paint: (p: PaintPoint) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return p.across > 0.8 ? 3 : 0;
    // Eye patch: an oval behind the eye on both flanks, drawn a little larger than life (s 0.11–0.25,
    // across 0.3 to the edge) so it still reads as the orca's mark at a few cells per body width.
    const ds = (p.s - 0.18) / 0.07;
    const da = (p.across - 0.68) / 0.38;
    if (ds * ds + da * da <= 1) return 1;
    // Saddle: grey where across is under a soft edge that is widest at its centre (s 0.525).
    if (p.s >= 0.45 && p.s <= 0.6 && p.across < 0.7 - (0.3 * Math.abs(p.s - 0.525)) / 0.075) {
      return 2;
    }
    return 0;
  },
};

/** Killer whale: a robust torpedo with a blunt head, big paddle flippers, a tall dorsal and a black-and-white coat. */
export const ORCA: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 7,
  taxon: "mammal",
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Blunt head: fills out fast, then swells to the broadest point near 0.3 of the length.
      if (s < 0.02) return 0.05 + 0.01 * smoothstep(0, 0.02, s);
      if (s < 0.12) return 0.06 + 0.04 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.12, s);
      // anatomy: [est. 1] widest near the front at ~0.2 TL full width (the research estimate is ~0.18).
      if (s < 0.3) return 0.1;
      // Long taper into a slim tail stock, then a thin peduncle to the flukes.
      if (s < 0.84) return 0.1 - 0.072 * Math.pow((s - 0.3) / 0.54, 1.2);
      return 0.028 - 0.008 * smoothstep(0.84, 1, s);
    },
    pectoral: [0.14, 0.24, 0.32, 0.26],
    paddle: true,
    falcate: false,
    pelvic: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    dorsal: [0.36, 0.45],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.1, 0.12],
    gills: [0, 0],
    tint: [1, 1, 1],
    // anatomy: [est. 1] fluke span ~0.24 TL (half-span 0.12).
    tail: { kind: "fluke", span: 0.12, length: 0.17, notch: 0.03 },
    undulation: 0.15,
    finFlap: 0.03,
  },
  coats: { orca: orcaCoat },
  defaultCoat: "orca",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/humpback.ts
```ts
import { hash3, smoothstep } from "../math";
import { blobs } from "./paint";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const BACK: Oklch = [0.3, 0.012, 250];
const FIN_WHITE: Oklch = [0.92, 0.006, 240];
const KNOB: Oklch = [0.2, 0.01, 250];
const BARNACLE: Oklch = [0.8, 0.01, 250];
/** A faint lighter blotch on the back, and the dorsal hump: low contrast on purpose. */
const MOTTLE: Oklch = [0.36, 0.012, 250];

/** Fine pale specks (barnacles) where a fine noise field runs high, so they cluster rather than scatter. */
function speck(p: PaintPoint, scale: number, salt: number, cut: number) {
  return blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, scale, salt) > cut;
}

/** Head tubercles: a sparse grid of cells 0.03 L square; a knob sits near each cell's hashed centre. Only on the head. */
function tubercle(p: PaintPoint) {
  if (p.s >= 0.2 || p.across >= 0.8) return false;
  const cell = 0.03 * p.L;
  const cx = (p.s * p.L) / cell;
  const cy = p.v / cell;
  const i = Math.floor(cx);
  const j = Math.floor(cy);
  // Hashed centre, kept inside its cell so a knob never straddles two cells.
  const hx = i + 0.2 + 0.6 * hash3(i, j, p.id * 3 + 11);
  const hy = j + 0.2 + 0.6 * hash3(i, j, p.id * 3 + 12);
  const dx = cx - hx;
  const dy = cy - hy;
  return dx * dx + dy * dy < 0.25 * 0.25;
}

/**
 * Humpback, dark back with huge white flippers. The flippers are freckled, with a knobbly leading
 * edge; the head is studded with tubercles and barnacles, and two blowhole marks sit behind it. The back is
 * faintly mottled, with a lighter hump. Flukes are back-coloured (seen from above), with a pale serrated trailing edge.
 */
const humpbackCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [BACK, FIN_WHITE, KNOB, BARNACLE, MOTTLE],
  paint: (p) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) {
      // The flukes: a pale serrated trailing edge, alternating every 0.015 of the length, and a few barnacles on the edges.
      if (p.s > 0.84) {
        if (p.s > 0.9 && p.across > 0.6 && Math.floor(p.v / (0.015 * p.L)) % 2 === 0) return 1;
        if (p.across > 0.85 && speck(p, 0.012, 19, 0.8)) return 3;
        return 0;
      }
      // Only the flippers are white; other fins keep the back colour.
      if (p.s < 0.22 || p.s > 0.52) return 0;
      // Barnacles cluster on the flipper tips and along the leading edge.
      if ((p.s > 0.44 || p.across > 0.85) && speck(p, 0.012, 13, 0.8)) return 3;
      // Knobbly leading edge: the outermost cells alternate dark every 0.02 of the length.
      if (p.across > 0.85 && Math.floor(p.s / 0.02) % 2 === 0) return 0;
      // Freckles.
      if (blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.03, 9) > 0.82) return 0;
      return 1;
    }
    // The blowhole splashguard: a short dark pair either side of the centre line, just behind the snout.
    const vl = Math.abs(p.v) / p.L;
    if (Math.abs(p.s - 0.13) < 0.016 && vl > 0.012 && vl < 0.04) return 2;
    if (tubercle(p)) return 2;
    // Barnacles on the head.
    if (p.s < 0.2 && speck(p, 0.015, 17, 0.78)) return 3;
    // The dorsal hump is a touch lighter than the back, so its ridge reads.
    if (p.s > 0.6 && p.s < 0.68 && p.across < 0.25) return 4;
    // Faint lighter mottling across the back, on a large scale.
    if (p.s > 0.2 && p.s < 0.8 && blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.2, 21) > 0.62) return 4;
    return 0;
  },
};

/** Humpback whale: a long, robust body with a broad flat head, very long flippers, and a small dorsal hump. */
export const HUMPBACK: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 14,
  taxon: "mammal",
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Rostrum, then the broad flat head widens to its full depth by 0.15.
      if (s < 0.15) return 0.05 + 0.05 * smoothstep(0.01, 0.15, s);
      if (s < 0.3) return 0.1 + 0.025 * smoothstep(0.15, 0.3, s);
      if (s < 0.45) return 0.125 + 0.005 * smoothstep(0.3, 0.45, s);
      // Long taper into the tail stock, then a thin peduncle to the flukes.
      if (s < 0.84) return 0.13 - 0.1 * Math.pow((s - 0.45) / 0.39, 1.2);
      return 0.03 - 0.008 * smoothstep(0.84, 1, s);
    },
    // Very long flippers, about 30% of the length, swept back like wings.
    pectoral: [0.22, 0.29, 0.52, 0.3],
    paddle: true,
    falcate: false,
    pelvic: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    // A small hump two-thirds of the way back.
    dorsal: [0.62, 0.66],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.16, 0.18],
    gills: [0, 0],
    tint: [1, 1, 1],
    // anatomy: [est. 1] fluke span 0.25–0.3 TL (half-span 0.14).
    tail: { kind: "fluke", span: 0.14, length: 0.15, notch: 0.025 },
    undulation: 0.1,
    finFlap: 0.05,
    finFlapRate: 0.35,
  },
  coats: { humpback: humpbackCoat },
  defaultCoat: "humpback",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/roster.ts
```ts
import type { Rng } from "../math";
import type { SharkSpec } from "../world";
import { CREATURES } from "./index";
import type { CreatureDef, CreatureGroup, CreatureSpecies, OceanScale } from "./types";

/** One animal to place in the water. `coat` names its colour scheme, for species that have coats. */
export type RosterEntry = { species: CreatureSpecies; rel: number; depth: number; coat?: string };

/** Most animals of one species a single group may place. */
export const MAX_PER_GROUP = 12;
/** Most animals in the whole roster. */
export const MAX_CREATURES = 16;
/** Exponent that compresses real length ratios; `stylized` softens them, `true` keeps them. */
export const SCALE_EXPONENT: Record<OceanScale, number> = { stylized: 0.45, true: 1 };

const JITTER = 0.16;

/**
 * Turns a group's variety into a coat for the species. A string is the coat (or the default when it is
 * unknown); an array is returned as the names to pick from, one per animal; no variety gives the default.
 */
function resolveCoat(
  species: CreatureSpecies,
  def: CreatureDef,
  variety: string | string[] | undefined,
  warnings: string[],
): string | string[] | undefined {
  const coats = def.coats;
  if (!coats) return undefined;
  const fallback = def.defaultCoat;
  if (typeof variety === "string") {
    if (Object.hasOwn(coats, variety)) return variety;
    warnings.push(`${species} variety "${variety}" is unknown; using ${fallback ?? "no coat"}`);
    return fallback;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(variety)) {
    const known: string[] = [];
    for (const name of variety) {
      if (Object.hasOwn(coats, name)) known.push(name);
      else warnings.push(`${species} variety "${name}" is unknown; skipped`);
    }
    if (known.length > 0) return known;
    return fallback === undefined ? undefined : [fallback];
  }
  return fallback;
}

/** Legacy `sharks` prop: length = base × size, depth = size. No randomness. */
export function rosterFromSharks(sharks: SharkSpec[]): RosterEntry[] {
  return sharks.map((s) => ({ species: s.kind, rel: s.size, depth: s.size }));
}

/**
 * `creatures` prop. rel = (realLength / largest realLength in the roster)^exponent × size × jitter,
 * jitter = 1 + (rng() − 0.5) × 0.16 (±8%). depth = rel / largest rel (so the biggest is nearest).
 * Counts are clamped to 1–MAX_PER_GROUP and the total to MAX_CREATURES; returns the warnings it raised.
 */
export function rosterFromGroups(
  groups: CreatureGroup[],
  scale: OceanScale,
  rng: Rng,
): { entries: RosterEntry[]; warnings: string[] } {
  const warnings: string[] = [];
  // Pass 1: settle counts, sizes and coats, so the largest animal is known before any rng call.
  const planned: { species: CreatureSpecies; size: number; coat?: string | string[] }[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  for (const group of groups) {
    const { species } = group;
    if (!Object.hasOwn(CREATURES, species)) {
      warnings.push(`unknown species "${species}"; skipped`);
      continue;
    }
    let count = group.count ?? 1;
    if (!Number.isFinite(count)) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} is not finite; using 1`);
      count = 1;
    } else if (!Number.isInteger(count)) {
      const floored = Math.floor(count);
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} is not an integer; using ${floored}`);
      count = floored;
    }
    if (count < 1) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count below 1; using 1`);
      count = 1;
    }
    if (count > MAX_PER_GROUP) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} exceeds ${MAX_PER_GROUP}; using ${MAX_PER_GROUP}`);
      count = MAX_PER_GROUP;
    }
    const room = MAX_CREATURES - total;
    if (count > room) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} exceeds the ${MAX_CREATURES} total; using ${room}`);
      count = room;
    }

    let size = group.size ?? 1;
    if (!Number.isFinite(size) || size <= 0) {
      warnings.push(`${species} size ${size} must be a positive number; using 1`);
      size = 1;
    }

    const coat = resolveCoat(species, CREATURES[species], group.variety, warnings);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) planned.push({ species, size, coat });
    total += count;
  }

  if (planned.length === 0) return { entries: [], warnings };

  const exponent = SCALE_EXPONENT[scale];
  const largest = Math.max(...planned.map((p) => CREATURES[p.species].realLength));

  // Pass 2: one rng call per animal, in roster order. A coat list takes a second call after the jitter.
  const entries: RosterEntry[] = planned.map((p) => {
    const jitter = 1 + (rng() - 0.5) * JITTER;
    const rel = (CREATURES[p.species].realLength / largest) ** exponent * p.size * jitter;
    const coat = Array.isArray(p.coat) ? p.coat[Math.floor(rng() * p.coat.length)] : p.coat;
    return { species: p.species, rel, depth: rel, coat };
  });

  const nearest = Math.max(...entries.map((e) => e.rel));
  for (const e of entries) e.depth = e.rel / nearest;

  return { entries, warnings };
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/behaviour.ts
```ts
import type { CreatureSpecies } from "./types";

/** How a species swims while cruising: pod, pointer and turning limits. Species without an entry use DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR. */
export interface Behaviour {
  /** Drifts after the pointer when it hovers nearby (koi come up to people at the pond edge). */
  followsPointer: boolean;
  /** Keeps company with its own species: cohesion and alignment toward pod mates. */
  pod?: boolean;
  /** Most bend per spine joint (radians) in follow-the-leader; default DEFAULT_MAX_BEND. */
  maxBend?: number;
  /** Smallest turning radius in body lengths while cruising; default DEFAULT_TURN_RADIUS. */
  turnRadius?: number;
}

/** Default most bend per spine joint (radians). A wide joint limit with a gentle arc keeps bodies from curling into a C. */
export const DEFAULT_MAX_BEND = 0.1;
/** Default smallest turning radius in body lengths. Wider than the classic shark agility, so turns are arcs, not pivots. */
export const DEFAULT_TURN_RADIUS = 0.6;
/** Turning radius (body lengths) for an animal whose snout is past the canvas edge: it turns back at the old classic agility. */
export const EDGE_TURN_RADIUS = 0.22;

export const DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR: Behaviour = {
  followsPointer: false,
};

export const BEHAVIOURS: Partial<Record<CreatureSpecies, Behaviour>> = {
  koi: {
    followsPointer: true,
    maxBend: 0.09,
    turnRadius: 0.5,
  },
  // Dolphins and whales travel in pods.
  bottlenose: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, pod: true },
  orca: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, pod: true },
};

/** How a species swims. Species without an entry use DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR. */
export function behaviourOf(species: CreatureSpecies): Behaviour {
  return BEHAVIOURS[species] ?? DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/world.ts
```ts
// Ported from https://github.com/stackedlol/ascii-sharks (used with the author's permission).
/**
 * Artificial-life sim for the hero: bait fish schools and the animals that swim among them.
 *
 * Fish: Reynolds boids (separation / alignment / cohesion) in Couzin-style
 * zones, with a blind spot behind each fish. Alarm spreads from fish to fish
 * through the school (a "wave of agitation"), so a whole school bolts even
 * when only its edge can see a predator. Fleeing fish break sideways around
 * the predator's path, which gives the fountain effect seen in real bait balls.
 * A fish that swims off the canvas is replaced by a new school from an edge.
 *
 * Animals: Reynolds steering (wander + pod cohesion + patrol) on a
 * follow-the-leader spine. They only swim; nothing hunts or feeds.
 */
import { angleDiff, clamp, createRng, current, rand, randInt, type Rng, TAU } from "./math";
import {
  behaviourOf,
  DEFAULT_MAX_BEND,
  DEFAULT_TURN_RADIUS,
  EDGE_TURN_RADIUS,
} from "./creatures/behaviour";
import { CREATURES } from "./creatures";
import { rosterFromSharks, type RosterEntry } from "./creatures/roster";
import { TAXA } from "./creatures/taxa";
import type { CreatureSpecies, OceanScene } from "./creatures/types";
import {
  advanceBreath,
  BLOWHOLE_S,
  type Blow,
  BREATH_PROFILES,
  type Breath,
  diveDepth,
  DRIFT_PERIOD,
  type Drift,
  driftOffset,
  startBreath,
} from "./creatures/vertical";
import { pickSpecies, SPECIES } from "./species";

export const SPINE_NODES = 20;

/** How fast the koi pond bob eases toward its target: d += (target − d) · min(1, dt · rate). */
const DIVE_RATE = 5;
/** Heading error gain (per second) while cruising: turn rate = gain · error, capped by the turning circle. */
const TURN_GAIN = 1.8;
/** Time constant (s) of the low-pass filter on an animal's steering direction, so terms don't flip the target each frame. */
const STEER_TAU = 0.4;
/** Pod mates keep less personal space than strangers: their separation weight is scaled by this. */
const POD_SEPARATION = 0.15;
/** How strongly a pod member turns to its pod's mean heading. */
const POD_ALIGN = 2.5;
/** Separation push strength, and its range in body lengths (sum of the two animals), between animals. */
const SEPARATION_K = 20;
const SEPARATION_RANGE = 0.9;

export type SharkKind =
  "classic" | "hammerhead" | "zebra" | "tiger" | "greathammer" | "mako" | "thresher" | "nurse";

/** Which sharks to put in the water; size is relative to the biggest. */
export type SharkSpec = { kind: SharkKind; size: number };

// The landing hero: four of the same shark at different depths.
const DEFAULT_SHARKS: SharkSpec[] = [
  { kind: "classic", size: 1 },
  { kind: "classic", size: 0.7 },
  { kind: "classic", size: 0.84 },
  { kind: "classic", size: 0.77 },
];

/** Constructor options for World: roster, seed, and population overrides. */
export type WorldOptions = {
  roster?: RosterEntry[];
  /** Same seed, same world. Omit for a different ocean each time. */
  seed?: number;
  /** Fish population: a multiple of the area-based default, "auto", or false for none. */
  baitFish?: number | "auto" | false;
  /** Exact fish count; overrides baitFish. */
  fishCount?: number;
  /** Ocean has bait fish; pond has koi and no fish by default. Default "ocean". */
  scene?: OceanScene;
};

export interface Fish {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  species: number;
  /** 0 calm → 1 panicking. Spreads to neighbours. */
  alarm: number;
  alive: boolean;
  respawnAt: number;
  /** Tail-beat phase. */
  phase: number;
  /** Smoothed angular velocity (rad/s); hard turns flash silver. */
  turn: number;
  /** 0.55 far → 1 near. */
  depth: number;
  /** Rotation sense shared by a school when it mills into a bait ball. */
  millDir: 1 | -1;
}

export interface Creature {
  id: number;
  species: CreatureSpecies;
  length: number;
  /** Spine positions, snout first: [x0, y0, x1, y1, ...]. */
  nodes: Float32Array;
  heading: number;
  speed: number;
  /** Tail-beat phase; drives the body wave in the renderer. */
  phase: number;
  /** Smoothed angular velocity (rad/s); the body banks its fins into turns. */
  turn: number;
  wander: number;
  orbitDir: 1 | -1;
  orbitRadius: number;
  /** 0 far / dim → 1 near / bright. */
  depth: number;
  /** Named coat from the species' `coats`, for species that have them. */
  coat?: string;
  /**
   * How deep the animal reads, 0 at the surface → 1 at depth. Pond koi bob between the two; a mammal dives (1),
   * rises and blows at the surface (0), then sinks. 0 for everything else.
   */
  dive: number;
  /** Mammals in the ocean: the breathing cycle. null for everything else. */
  breath: Breath | null;
  /** Fish in the ocean: a slow depth drift. null for everything else. */
  drift: Drift | null;
  /** The drift's current offset from `depth` (fish only, within ±DRIFT_AMP); the renderer adds it. 0 otherwise. */
  depthOffset: number;
  /** Low-passed steering direction (unit-ish vector, x and y): the target the heading turns toward. */
  steerX: number;
  steerY: number;
}

/** Things that happen in the water this step. The renderer turns them into rings, spray and wakes, then clears them. */
export type WorldEvent =
  | { kind: "splash"; x: number; y: number }
  /** An animal surfaces and blows at (x, y), its blowhole. `drops`, `reach` and `life` are the blow's spray. */
  | ({ kind: "blow"; x: number; y: number } & Blow);

const FISH_SEPARATION_K = 420;
const FISH_ALIGN_K = 1.4;
const FISH_COHESION_K = 1;
const FISH_FLEE_K = 1100;
const BLIND_COS = -0.6; // ~127° either side of the heading is visible
/** Fish beyond this many px outside the canvas have left it and are replaced by respawn(). */
const FISH_EXIT = 60;

/** Inward steer from an edge `depth` px inside it: 0 at half a length in, 2 at the edge and beyond. */
function edgePush(depth: number, L: number) {
  const margin = 0.5 * L;
  return 2 * clamp((margin - depth) / margin, 0, 1);
}

/** One eased step of the koi pond bob, snapped to exactly 0 when it is back at rest there. */
function easeDive(d: number, target: number, dt: number) {
  const v = d + (target - d) * Math.min(1, dt * DIVE_RATE);
  return target === 0 && Math.abs(v) < 1e-3 ? 0 : v;
}

export class World {
  width = 0;
  height = 0;
  time = 0;
  fish: Fish[] = [];
  creatures: Creature[] = [];
  /**
   * Events raised this step (splashes, blows). The renderer drains them on each render and clears the array.
   * A headless World (tests, the sim without a renderer) must drain it too, or it grows without limit.
   */
  events: WorldEvent[] = [];
  pointer = { x: 0, y: 0, active: false };
  scene: OceanScene;

  /** Fish neighbourhood radius; the spatial hash bucket size matches it. */
  private perception = 60;
  /** Per-species personal space in px (x, y). */
  private sepX: number[] = [];
  private sepY: number[] = [];
  private foreignSep = 40;
  private bucketCols = 0;
  private bucketRows = 0;
  private bucketHead = new Int32Array(0);
  private bucketNext = new Int32Array(0);
  private lastSpawn = 0;
  private tmp = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  private rng: Rng;
  private roster: RosterEntry[];
  private fishCount?: number;
  private baitFish: number | "auto" | false;

  /**
   * fishCount overrides the area-based population (the sidebar wants a single small school).
   * seed makes every random choice repeatable; omit it for a different ocean each time.
   */
  constructor(options: WorldOptions = {}) {
    this.roster = options.roster ?? rosterFromSharks(DEFAULT_SHARKS);
    this.fishCount = options.fishCount;
    this.baitFish = options.baitFish ?? "auto";
    this.rng = options.seed === undefined ? Math.random : createRng(options.seed);
    this.scene = options.scene ?? "ocean";
  }

  resize(width: number, height: number, cellW: number, cellH: number) {
    const first = this.width === 0;
    this.width = width;
    this.height = height;
    // Spacing is set in grid cells so every fish lands in its own cell.
    this.sepX = SPECIES.map((sp) => sp.spacing[0] * cellW);
    this.sepY = SPECIES.map((sp) => sp.spacing[1] * cellH);
    this.perception = Math.max(cellH * 3, cellW * 5.5);
    this.foreignSep = cellW * 3;

    if (first || (this.fish.length === 0 && this.creatures.length === 0)) {
      this.seed();
      return;
    }
    for (const f of this.fish) {
      f.x = clamp(f.x, 0, width);
      f.y = clamp(f.y, 0, height);
    }
  }

  seed() {
    const { width: W, height: H } = this;
    const minSide = Math.min(W, H);
    // A roster with a whale in it (realLength 7 or more, orca and humpback) lets the biggest animal be big.
    const whales = this.roster.some((e) => CREATURES[e.species].realLength >= 7);
    const baseLength = whales ? clamp(minSide * 0.6, 200, 520) : clamp(minSide * 0.44, 170, 430);
    const count = this.roster.length;

    this.creatures = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const entry = this.roster[i];
      const length = baseLength * entry.rel;
      const orbitRadius = minSide * rand(this.rng, 0.28, 0.4);
      const a = (i / count) * TAU + rand(this.rng, -0.4, 0.4);
      const orbitDir: 1 | -1 = i % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1;
      const x = W / 2 + Math.cos(a) * orbitRadius * (W / minSide) * 0.9;
      const y = H / 2 + Math.sin(a) * orbitRadius;
      const heading = a + (orbitDir * Math.PI) / 2;
      const nodes = new Float32Array(SPINE_NODES * 2);
      const seg = length / (SPINE_NODES - 1);
      for (let n = 0; n < SPINE_NODES; n++) {
```
